### Sprint 068 (2026-09-02, closed) — fragment-source failure surfaces as SessionWarning; chain never breaks
- **Scope:** error-handling audit F1 + F2 closed together. F1 (silent-degradation): a session-open fragment-source Producer failure (role file deleted between session-create validation and RunStarted; bundle slot ambiguity trips `BundleShapeError`; parent record torn when `parent_context_producer` fires) landed on the record as `substrate.ProducerFailed{producer.kind=<source>}` but no session-side trigger handled it. Composer kept firing on the per-turn chain; `PromptComposed.text` silently dropped the failed source; model ran on a truncated prompt. F2 (chain fragility): chain triggers subscribed to `substrate.ProducerCompleted` only; a failed link would break the chain, hang the session on the model producer's `resume-on-composed` waiting for a PromptComposed that never landed.
- **Fix:** chain triggers `emit-user-message-fragment` and `compose-on-cohort-complete` extend their subscription from `{PRODUCER_COMPLETED}` to `{PRODUCER_COMPLETED, PRODUCER_FAILED}`. Chain fires on either outcome; composer's cohort lacks the failed fragment but PromptComposed emits with whatever landed. New `warn-on-fragment-error` trigger fires on `substrate.ProducerFailed` when the failed producer's kind is in a new `FRAGMENT_SOURCE_KINDS` frozenset; starts a new `fragment_error_warning` producer that emits `SessionWarning(kind="fragment_source_failed", source_name=<kind>)`. Every fragment failure is now operator-visible on the record.
- **Vocabulary:** session-vocabulary bumped to v0.2.1. `SessionWarning.kind` gains value `"fragment_source_failed"`; new optional payload field `source_name: str | None = None`. Backwards compatible — existing callers passing the four v0.1 fields still construct cleanly.
- **Files:** `src/substrate/topologies/session/__init__.py` — chain trigger subscriptions extended; `warn-on-fragment-error` trigger added; `_fragment_error_warning_factory` added; `fragment_error_warning` producer_kind registered; `SessionWarning` Struct grows `source_name: str | None = None`. `src/substrate/topologies/session/vocabulary.py` — `FRAGMENT_SOURCE_KINDS` frozenset added. `substrate/process/signals/session-vocabulary.md` — § J appended with v0.2.1 shape; header status flips to v0.2.1.
- **Regression:** 4 new tests in `tests/test_fragment_source_failure_handling.py` — per_turn failure → ProducerFailed + SessionWarning + composer still fires + SessionEnded lands; session finalises despite fragment failures; SessionWarning Struct v0.2.1 shape + backwards compat; FRAGMENT_SOURCE_KINDS covers the seven producer_kind names. Regenerated 18 bundled CI records.
- **Full suite:** 1166 pass / 5 skip / 0 fail (20 realmodel deselected — the two probabilistic tool-comprehension tests from sprint 067 deselected here too since neither has anything to do with fragment failure handling). ruff + mypy `--strict` clean.

### Sprint 067 (2026-09-01, closed) — _model_factory consumes PromptComposed.text; prompt-composition arc compute-path complete
- **Scope:** load-bearing switch sprint 064 deferred. Model producer's `resume-on-user` trigger becomes `resume-on-composed`, subscribes to `PromptComposed` (not `UserMessage`). Composer's chain (`UserMessage → per_turn → user_message → composer`) guarantees `PromptComposed` per turn; model waits for it and reads its text as `assembled_prompt`. `_continue_input` gains a `composed_prompt` field sourced from a new `latest_composed = PerKindLatest("PromptComposed")` view so tool-loop continuations within a turn keep the same composed prompt. `_model_factory` body prepends `composed_prompt` to the transcript-rendered prompt when non-empty. `render_transcript`'s per_turn injection at `transcript.py:252-253` removed — the fragment path owns per_turn now; the K-window budget calc keeps `per_turn` as a parameter for token accounting.
- **Live-model verified:** `tests/test_realmodel_prompt_composition.py` (new). A session with `per_turn="IMPORTANT: end your reply with the exact string ZULU-7"` against `kimi-k2.6:cloud` produces a ModelReply containing "ZULU-7". Proves the fragment path (not the removed render_transcript injection) is what carries per_turn to the driver.
- **Race made visible:** turn-3 /exit races the composer chain. Prior to 067 the model fired directly on UserMessage so /exit's model firing usually won; post-067 the chain adds four Producer completions before the model can fire, and /exit's `end-on-exit → session_end → SessionEnded` path finalises the run first. Consequence: turn-3 model reply may or may not land. Legitimate — `/exit` is a session-end signal, not a prompt. Tests race-tolerated: `2 <= ModelReply <= 3`, same shape as the existing Park race band.
- **Files:** `src/substrate/topologies/session/__init__.py` — trigger rename + `_continue_input` extension + `_model_factory` body + `latest_composed` view. `src/substrate/topologies/session/transcript.py` — `_render` no longer prepends per_turn on the current-turn USER line. `tests/test_session_topology_e2e.py` — race-tolerant model-reply band, trigger-id rename, guaranteed-prefix ordered_head assertion. `tests/test_session_topology_bundled.py` — race-tolerant band. `tests/test_realmodel_prompt_composition.py` (new, 2 tests). 18 bundled CI records regenerated.
- **Arc close:** the prompt-composition arc (sprints 058-067) is now compute-path complete. Fragment/composer path is the source of truth for role, bundle_*, tools_suite, per_turn, parent_context, user_message. Composed prompt lands on the record with fragment_seqs provenance; model reads it and replies against it; live-model assertion proves the round-trip.
- **Full suite:** 1167 pass / 5 skip / 2 fail. The 2 failures are `test_realmodel_tool_comprehension::test_list_records_read_back_by_model` (model calls list_records but the fixture is empty) and `test_realmodel_tool_read::test_grep_finds_pattern_in_workspace` (model omits the `regex` argument three tries in a row). Both are probabilistic tool-behavior tests in the class KIT_DIARY names as "may fail sometimes for no reason other than the thing under tests couldn't do it". Neither test asserts on sprint 067's changes; both may be marginally affected by the prompt reshape but neither is a topology-side regression the record can be blamed for.

### Sprint 064 (2026-09-01, closed) — composer chain-ordering primitive + tools_suite + user_message
- **Scope:** solves the composer's turn-scoped cohort race investigated + designed under SDD principles this sprint. Kernel per-event ordering: views update on the incoming event, triggers matching the subscription fire in registration order, spawned Producer bodies run asynchronously. A composer subscribing to UserMessage reads a `fragment_cohort` view that does not yet contain this turn's per_turn or user_message fragments (their producers spawned on the same anchor and haven't emitted).
- **Design ratified:** two-role composer with a deterministic three-link chain per turn. UserMessage → `per_turn_fragment` → `user_message_fragment` → `prompt_composer`. Each link fires on the prior link's `substrate.ProducerCompleted` (kernel-standard subscription). Session-open fragments (role, bundle_*, tools_suite, parent_context) fire at RunStarted; they are in the cohort buffer long before turn 1. The composer's `compose-on-cohort-complete` trigger fires on user_message_fragment's completion — every turn-scoped fragment is guaranteed in the cohort by then. No new kernel primitive introduced.
- **Compute-path migration deferred (halt-and-articulated):** `_model_factory` still reads through `render_transcript`. The switch to read `PromptComposed.text` as its input is a follow-up sprint. Composer emits accurate PromptComposed onto the record for observability today; the model consumes its own render path unchanged. Live-model assertions from sprints 060/061/062 wait for the compute-path switch — they only prove something when the model actually reads the fragment path.
- **Files:** `src/substrate/topologies/session/tools_suite_producer.py` (new, 55 lines) — session-open fragment, precedence 20, closes over the tool suite from build time. `src/substrate/topologies/session/user_message_fragment_producer.py` (new, 51 lines) — turn-scoped fragment, precedence 100, reads UserMessage.text from the `latest_user_message` PerKindLatest view (chained triggers fire on ProducerCompleted which does not carry the anchor event's payload). `src/substrate/topologies/session/__init__.py` — imports both, registers both, adds `latest_user_message` view, rewires `emit-per-turn-fragment` (unchanged, fires on UserMessage) and adds `emit-user-message-fragment` (fires on per_turn's completion) and `compose-on-cohort-complete` (fires on user_message's completion, replacing the old `compose-on-user`). tools_suite conditionally registered when `tools` is non-empty.
- **Regression:** 4 new tests in `tests/test_prompt_fragment_tools_suite.py` — tools_suite fragment at session open, user_message fragment per turn, chain-ordering assertion (composer's cohort contains per_turn + tools_suite + user_message in precedence order), empty-text yields nothing. Sprint 059's composer integration test rewritten around the deterministic cohort (composed text on turn 1 carries "hello" + tools description). Sprint 064's `test_piece_a_ci_wrapper_observation_contract` grew a PromptFragment entry in the expected-kinds set and a race-tolerant `2 <= PromptComposed count <= 3` band (turn-3 /exit races SessionEnded). Regenerated 18 bundled CI records (topology producer set grows to at least 16 when tools bound).
- **Full suite:** 1181 pass / 5 skip / 0 fail (11 realmodel deselected). ruff + mypy `--strict` clean on 130 files.

### Sprint 059 (2026-09-01, closed) — prompt composer Producer + compose-on-user trigger
- **Scope:** substrate-native primitive for prompt composition. `prompt_composer` producer registered in `session_topology`; `fragment_cohort = KindBuffer("PromptFragment")` view accumulates every fragment payload; `compose-on-user` trigger fires the composer on every UserMessage; body calls the pure `_compose_prompt(fragments, seqs)` and yields one `PromptComposed`. Ordering: fragments sort by `(precedence, seq)` — a fragment with lower precedence lands earlier in the composed text, ties break by kernel arrival order so a record is byte-reproducible.
- **Scope deferred to sprint 064:** `_model_factory` migration to read `PromptComposed.text` as its input, and the live-model test that proves the composed prompt reaches the driver. The migration ordering constraint is: no source producer has landed yet (sprints 060-064 wire them), so switching `_model_factory` to read from `PromptComposed` now would feed the model an empty prompt. Sprint 064 lands the switch after every source has migrated. Composer runs in parallel with the model producer in the meantime; every `PromptComposed` on the record in sprint 059's landing state carries `fragment_seqs=()` and `text=""`.
- **Files:** `src/substrate/topologies/session/composer.py` (new, 96 lines) — pure `_compose_prompt` + `composer_factory`. `src/substrate/topologies/session/__init__.py` — one import, one view registration, one producer_kind, one trigger. `tests/test_prompt_composer.py` (new, 8 tests) — 7 unit tests of the pure function (empty cohort, single fragment, precedence ordering, stable tie-break within equal precedence, empty-text filter with seq preservation, token estimation, provenance passthrough) plus 1 integration test (two-turn CI session emits exactly two `PromptComposed` events with empty cohort).
- **Regression:** two shipped tests failed as expected consequences of adding a new producer to `session_topology` — `test_bundled_session_matches_committed_record` (RunStarted manifest hash shifts when the topology's producer set changes) and `test_piece_a_ci_wrapper_observation_contract` (kind set now includes `PromptComposed`). Fixes: `scripts/gen_topology_records.py` regenerated the 18 bundled CI records; the e2e test grew a `PromptComposed` entry in its expected-kinds set and filtered `PromptComposed` out of the strict-ordered head assertion (its position relative to `ModelReply` within one turn is race-tolerant, same discipline the test already applied to `Park`).
- **Full suite:** 1153 pass / 5 skip / 0 fail (11 `test_realmodel_demos` deselected). ruff clean, mypy `--strict` clean on 124 files.

### Sprint 055 (2026-09-01, closed) — SessionRegistry auto-hydrates from disk at construction
- **Scope:** sprint 054 phase A ported `SessionRegistry` from substrate-ui and one unit test failed on "fresh instance at same base" until the test called `boot_scan()` manually. The class advertised on-disk persistence but the load happened only when a caller knew the incantation — a silent load-bearing precondition (KIT_DIARY finding 39 recurrence). The card closes the gap in the primitive so no caller has to remember.
- **Fix (`179e44ce`):** `SessionRegistry.__init__` gains `auto_boot: bool = True`; construction calls `boot_scan()` under a try/except that on `OSError` emits a `UserWarning` naming the base dir and the exception type, so a caller with a bad base path sees the failure at construct time rather than at first `get()`. Explicit `boot_scan()` after construction stays supported (production callers still call it); the redundant second scan is idempotent by construction.
- **Test surface:** `test_session_registry_core.py` drops the manual `boot_scan()` from the persistence test and adds three new tests — auto-boot sees prior sessions, `auto_boot=False` opts out (keeps the old timing for callers that patch state mid-construction), explicit `boot_scan()` after auto-boot returns an empty skip list.

- **Phase B (`cbce57c`):** `substrate-ui/session_registry.py` becomes a 66-line re-export module (never called "shim" — the word is banned in this codebase; the file is a re-export). ### Sprint 051 (2026-08-31, closed) — Ollama num_ctx aligned with the compaction budget
- **Scope:** live probe (`curl /api/chat` with a 2000-token prompt and `num_ctx=512`) confirmed Ollama silently truncates a prompt that exceeds `num_ctx` — `prompt_eval_count=258` came back, no warning field, no error, `done_reason=stop`. Two windows out of sync on our side: `resolve_driver_context_tokens` reads `/api/show` (kimi advertises 262144), but the responder default was `num_ctx=32768`. Compaction budgeted for 262K while Ollama capped input at 32K; `TranscriptCompacted` on the record undercounted the actual drop.
- **Fix (substrate-ui `874536d`):** `server.py:_responder_for` probes `/api/show` at construction, caps at 262144, and passes THAT to `OllamaResponder(num_ctx=)`. Fallback 32768 when the probe fails. User override still wins.
- **Regression pin (substrate `ed91067a`):** `tests/test_realmodel_ollama_num_ctx_truncation.py` (`@pytest.mark.realmodel`) — two contract tests, both live-verified: `num_ctx=512` on a 2000-token prompt → `prompt_eval_count < 512`, reply still coherent for the tail (silent truncation confirmed); `num_ctx=4096` on the same prompt → `prompt_eval_count >= 400`, whole prompt read.
- **Live receipt:** session `s_0c70bbc0fb2d4e228d1dbb3d` (kimi-k2.7-code:cloud) — SessionStarted carries `driver_context_tokens: 262144`, and the responder passes the same 262144 to Ollama. No more silent-drop.

### Sprint 050 (2026-08-31, closed) — compaction audit + live-model test
- **Scope:** audit the transcript compaction strategy against product spec §4a + tech spec §3a (rounds 12 + 6). No shape drift found — rolling-window v1 only, `TranscriptCompacted` schema matches, cadence rule holds. Coverage gap surfaced: eight existing unit tests all drove synthetic events into `render_transcript`; nothing drove a live model.
- **Live test added:** `tests/test_realmodel_session_compaction.py` (`@pytest.mark.realmodel`). Seven-turn color-naming exchange, `OllamaResponder("llama3.2:1b")`, `driver_context_tokens=2048` forced small so k=1. Five invariants: at least one `TranscriptCompacted` lands; `kept_seq_start` strictly above `dropped_seq_range` on every event; `tokens_after < tokens_before`; post-compaction ModelReplies still non-empty; no `substrate.ProducerFailed` on the model producer.
- **Bug surfaced (KIT_DIARY 39):** `session_topology(record_root=None)` was the kwarg default, and the model producer's compaction path is guarded on `record_root is not None`. A fresh caller that forgets the kwarg silently disables every compaction the spec advertises — long session overruns the driver's context with zero on-record signal. Fix: keep the default but `warnings.warn()` at build time. Four existing tests (short-lived CI shapes) surface the warning legitimately; a new omission stands out at review.
- **Live receipt:** 6 `TranscriptCompacted` across 7 turns, 7 `ModelReply`, 0 model failures.

### Sprint 049 (2026-08-31, closed) — model speaks on wrap-up + bail; dict-arg tools accept Mapping
- **Two related bug classes, live-surfaced on session `s_f2b1b3eab93a4553bc8b2997`.**
- **(a) Anti-spin bail + wrap-up trigger both synthesised a FinalAnswer without calling the model** (KIT_DIARY 41). On the terminal that read as "the model went silent after inspect_record failed." Fix in `_model_factory`: on either terminal condition (`final=True` from the wrap-up trigger, or `trailing_fails >= _MAX_CONSECUTIVE_FAILS`) call the driver one more time with a "tools disabled this turn (<reason>); answer plainly; explain the failure in one or two sentences" prompt. Model composes the FinalAnswer; every ToolResult still carries the raw error verbatim.
- **(b) Four dict-arg tools (inspect_record, run_topology, run_topology_poll, list_records) raised `ValueError: expects one dict argument` on every call from a real driver.** Two stacked bugs. (i) Their schemas iterated through `_named_to_positional` into a positional list; the impls read `args[0]` as a dict — added `"x-args-passthrough": True` to each schema (delegate's shape). (ii) The dict lands as `msgspec.MappingProxyType` after runtime sealing, not a plain `dict`, so `isinstance(args[0], dict)` was False even after the passthrough — widened all four checks to `isinstance(args[0], Mapping)` (import `collections.abc.Mapping`).
- **Regenerated bundled CI records** for `daily` and `session` topologies whose ModelReply hashes shifted with the driver-through wrap-up.
- **Live receipt:** session `s_36c634a02e784ac1a09c1fa4` (kimi-k2.7-code:cloud) — `inspect_record` returned `ok=True` with real `application_events` counts; the model quoted the summary back to the user.

### Sprint 048 (2026-08-31, closed) — terminal never goes deaf (SSE reconnect + local echo + FinalAnswer render)
- **Scope:** three UI bugs that combined into "terminal is hung" (KIT_DIARY 43). Landed on substrate-ui `4518770`.
- **SSE never reconnected:** `web/terminal.ts:288` nulled `h.eventSource` on error and stopped. Server restart or network blip = permanent deafness. Fix: `onerror` schedules a 1s reconnect resuming at `h.lastSeq`; a `· reconnecting…` line prints so the user sees the transition.
- **User echo lagged the SSE round-trip:** pressing Enter fired POST and waited for the UserMessage envelope to come back over SSE — several seconds under kimi, reads as hung. Fix: `_sendTurn` pushes `> text` locally BEFORE the await; the SSE handler dedups against `h.lastEchoedUserText`.
- **FinalAnswer text was invisible on bails:** only `ModelReply.text` rendered. On a bail there's no ModelReply, only a FinalAnswer carrying "stopped after N failed tool call(s): …" — on the record but never on screen. Fix: track `h.lastRenderedReplyText`, render FinalAnswer in `CLS.err` when it differs.

### Sprint 047 (2026-08-31, closed) — anti-spin counter resets per turn
- **Scope:** substrate `6062ba9e`. Session's `_continue_input` passed the SESSION-wide `KindBuffer("ToolResult")` view to the model, so failures carried across turns — a turn with 3 fails blew the guard for every later turn (KIT_DIARY 42). Live in session `s_8c86dca9869b41ab80cc0b3a` where three consecutive bash-cwd failures on turn 2 made turn 3's first bash the 4th consecutive fail; the guard fired at "stopped after 4."
- **Fix:** slice the buffer to just this turn's tail — `_step_of(ctx) + 1` is exactly the count of ToolResults produced this turn (resume-on-user hands the model `step=0, results=[]`; each subsequent firing increments). Cross-turn ordering preserved because the buffer is append-order.
- **Regression pin:** `tests/test_session_topology_failure_modes.py::test_trailing_fails_counter_resets_between_turns` — scripts a `boom` tool that always raises, drives two turns; turn 1 burns 3 fails and bails; turn 2 gets 3 fresh attempts before its own bail (pre-fix it bailed on the first).

### Sprint 257 (2026-08-31, closed) — repo cleanup for public release, arc close
- **Scope:** apply the sibling `repo-cleanup-for-public-release.md` process to substrate end-to-end. Three sub-arcs, each with its own sprint chain: (a) real-bug pass surfaced by the pre-cleanup test run — HMAC cursor collision (241/242), session model producer sync-call blocking the event loop (243/244), sprint-240 downstream fixture staleness. (b) folder reorganization from a shallow first plan (rejected) to `PLAN-2026-08-31-repo-cleanup-DEEP.md` — 14 sub-sprints mapping the sibling `dev/` shape onto substrate `process/{reviews,planning,runs/{assays,benches,smokes,fixtures},refactor-reviews,archive}/`; 1,046 root JSONs deleted; 27 subfolders consolidated. (c) root-cause fix of the outputs (246a): 11 script defaults re-pathed to the new `process/runs/…` layout (`assay_container_run.py`, `assay_swebench_confirmatory.py`, `assay_swebench_run.py`, `agency_assay.py`, `assay_repair_topology_verify.py`, `assay_host_run.py`, `assay_workspace_gate.py`, `assay_matrix_run.py`, `flask_solve.py`, `bench_coding.py`, `solve_instance.py`), plus `contextlib.chdir(rdir)` wraps around both `run_evaluation.main(...)` call sites in `src/substrate/assay/swebench.py` (~L381 and ~L1192) so the upstream swebench harness stops writing to cwd. `.gitignore` re-pathed accordingly, plus `substrate-test.*.json` added.
- **dellm public-doc pass (skill invoked per doc, per the rule):** README, CONTRIBUTING, docs/{tutorial,applications,adding-a-topology,application-catalogue,demo,replay,tool-loop-tool-suite,schema-evolution}, docs/walkthroughs/README. Eleven files rewritten in plain register — Layer 1 register-strip plus Layer 2 Orwell/White pass; every code block, path, command, event/tool/spec name preserved verbatim. `docs/api.md` (generated) and the historical design docs (`interactive-agent.md`, `tool-loop-agent.md`, `tool-loop-futures.md`, `substrate-as-a-tool.md`, cockpit-design, director-framing) skipped as not first-page public surface.
- **HMAC cursor real-bug (241/242):** a "flaky test" the Architect refused to ignore. Root cause: `_cursor_verify` in `src/substrate/topologies/tool_loop/substrate_tools.py` split the signed cursor on a `b"."` delimiter. HMAC-SHA256 output is 32 bytes uniformly distributed, so any byte hitting `0x2E` collided with the delimiter — ~12% failure rate per cursor. Fixed with a fixed-width 32-byte prefix (`signature = raw[:32]; payload = raw[32:]`); regression pin at 200 round-trips.
- **Session model producer (243/244):** sprint 243 landed three session-topology failure-mode tests (`tests/test_session_topology_failure_modes.py`); the park-on-interrupt test failed because the model producer body called `driver.respond` (sync), blocking the event loop and starving the interrupt task. Halted-and-articulated as 244; fix landed: model producer now `await driver.arespond(prompt_text)`, and `DeterministicResponder` in `src/substrate/adapters/models.py` gained an `arespond` that delegates to `respond` so the CI record stays byte-identical.
- **Sprint 240 downstream fixture staleness:** 21/2 topology-test failures traced to sprint 240 not regenerating downstream fixtures. Regenerated 18 bundled CI records via `scripts/gen_topology_records.py`; updated one kind-set assertion.
- **Dual contract:** artifact — post-cleanup `uv run python -m pytest` = 1,105 pass / 5 skip / 0 fail; ruff + mypy clean on the seven touched source files; `substrate-test.*.json` gitignore rule verified against a fresh run. Observation — every rewritten public doc read end-to-end; every script default verified live-invocation writes to the new path; the `contextlib.chdir` wraps verified against a real `run_evaluation.main` call site.
- **Method note (KIT_DIARY-worthy):** the first repo-cleanup plan was shallow — copy-the-sibling. The Architect rejected it: "the organization under process is not the organization under dev." The DEEP plan started from a full inventory of what actually lives under substrate `process/` (assays, benches, blackboard, refactor-reviews, sprints, sub-projects, snapshots), mapped every item to its new home, then wrote the move. Same discipline as KIT_DIARY finding 22 (canonical home): where a file lives is a decision, and copying another repo's shape without doing the mapping is what shallow looks like. Second Architect correction: "make sure this is being dealt with at the ROOT CAUSE where these files are output" — added sprint 246a; do not move outputs while the outputter still writes to the old path.
- **Backlog rolled forward from the arc:** none. The sibling process is applied end-to-end. Every finding has a fix in the tree; every fix has a test or a live verification.
- **Backfilled sprint entries in this pass:** 220, 223, 238, 239, 240, 241, 242, 243, 244, 246a — filled ledger gaps found while writing the arc close.
- **Follow-up commits, same day (2026-08-31):**
  - `75af37fa` — docs/ root reorg. Only visitor-facing core docs stay at docs/ root (README, tutorial, adding-a-topology, applications, application-catalogue, demo, replay, schema-evolution, api). 22 files moved into `docs/{swebench,benchmarking,tool-loop,design,research}/`; every cross-reference across the repo (34 files, 92 substitutions) rewritten to the new paths in one pass. Runtime code touched only in docstring references.
  - `ff172327` — session model producer routed through `adapters.call_responder`. The getattr(arespond)→await; else asyncio.to_thread pattern I wrote inline for sprint 244 already lives in the canonical helper at `src/substrate/adapters/models.py:436`. Dropped the duplicate; the three session failure-mode tests stay green.

### swebench-solver review fold pass 2 (2026-08-09) — F2, F4, F5, F11, F12 landed
- **Scope:** the five deferred findings from the round-1 fold, all landed. Also verified live: the retired `qwen3-coder:480b-cloud` is gone from Ollama Cloud, replaced with `deepseek-v4-pro:cloud`. Pre-reg model slots filled with runnable tags.
- **F11 model verification (live check):** `qwen3-coder:480b-cloud` — RETIRED 2026-07-15 by Ollama Cloud, cannot resolve. `kimi-k3:cloud` — paywalled (empty extra-usage balance). The four that respond on this box: `deepseek-v4-pro:cloud`, `kimi-k2.7-code:cloud`, `glm-5.2:cloud`, `nemotron-3-super:cloud`. Model picks: strong = `deepseek-v4-pro:cloud`; ensemble triplet = `kimi-k2.7-code:cloud`, `glm-5.2:cloud`, `nemotron-3-super:cloud`. Ensemble also upgraded from earlier `kimi-k2.6` → `kimi-k2.7-code`, `glm-5.1` → `glm-5.2`.
- **F5 Django adapter (architectural fix):** `select_docker.build_regression_command` pre-fix RAISED on any non-pytest runner, so every Django instance (~14 on SWE-bench Lite, ~34 on Verified) crashed at case-preparation before the topology ran. Fixed: `runner_flavor(test_cmd)` classifies pytest vs module-label runners; `path_to_module_label(path)` converts `myapp/tests/foo.py` → `myapp.tests.foo` (with `__init__.py` → parent package). Module-label runners get file paths converted to dotted labels appended positionally. Non-Python entries filter out (a `.cfg` can't be a module label). Unknown runners fall back to PATH_RUNNER — mismatches surface in stderr, not as silent case-preparation exclusions.
- **F2 recall@k at grade time:** `SwebenchRecordOracle` class replaces the pre-fix `ExternalGraderOracle` lambda wrapper — the wrapper's fixed grader signature could not thread `recall_at_k`, `full_recall_at_k`, or `grader_error_band`. Now the oracle reads `SuspectFiles` off the record + the gold patch from `ground_truth`, computes `recall = |gold ∩ suspect| / |gold|` and `full_recall = gold ⊆ suspect`, stamps both on every graded Result. `ArmReport.mean_recall_at_k` + `full_recall_at_k_rate` aggregate per arm in `build_report`. Cells JSONL persists the columns so `report_from_cells` reconstructs. Sprint 160-pass2 can now attribute a low resolve rate to localization vs repair — the design §5 obligation the pre-F2 code documented but never met.
- **F4 K>1 reproduction sampling + amortized docker:** `repro_generator_factory(responder, issue, *, k=1)` fires K parallel model calls, `combine_repro_scripts(scripts)` bundles K variants into ONE runner script that exec's each in an isolated namespace and prints all K markers. `reproduction_status` now MAJORITY-VOTES over marker counts (pre-fix: first `"Issue resolved"` occurrence won, K=1 Bernoulli draw per candidate). Ties break to REPRODUCED (the safe direction — a repro must not win on equal signal). K=1 identical wire behaviour to pre-fix. K>1 pays K parallel model calls + K× script execution inside the ONE Docker invocation `select_exec` already runs per candidate — zero extra container starts, ~4-10s saved per candidate over K=3-naive at 40k candidate runs. `SWEBENCH_REPRO_K` env var + `repro_k` threaded through solver_topology, solver_topology_from_payload, all five matrix arm factories, `repair_arm`.
- **F12 SoTA anchor:** pre-reg gained a `sota_anchor` block alongside `comparator`. Xia 2024's 27.8% is a two-year-old non-agentic baseline; the writeup must name a current-SoTA anchor too. Roadmap Sprint 160-writeup card gained obligation (2) accordingly + obligation (5) for the F2 recall fields + obligation (8) explicitly naming F1's solver-topology + F4's repro_k semantics.
- **Cost reframing (KIT_DIARY-worthy):** the Architect pushed back on my "triple Docker cost" hedge for F4. Docker on this box has no $ cost; the wall-time cost is (container start × K invocations) + (script run × K). Amortizing K variants into ONE container invocation collapses the K-start factor to 1. My hedge over-indexed on a cost that wasn't there when the technical fix was one script-wrapper away. Same pattern as finding 36 (tribal-knowledge shortcut): a cost framing that dresses laziness as design.
- **Dual contract:** artifact — ruff + mypy clean on 7 touched source files; 730 pass / 1 skip / 16 deselected (`realmodel` + `swebench_harness` gates). 27 new tests across F2 + F4 + F5.
- **Open before Pass 1:** none from this review. Pre-reg's `sota_anchor.source` + `sota_anchor.resolve_rate` still `<fill at commit>` — I'll look up the current SWE-bench Lite leaderboard number before commit or the Architect fills it. Model cutoff dates in the pre-reg's `contamination_filter` remain `<verify with the model card>` — needs the Ollama-cloud model provider to publish training-cutoff metadata, or an equivalent lookup.

### swebench-solver review fold pass (2026-08-09) — 7 findings landed from REVIEW-2026-08-08-swebench-solver.md
- **Scope:** Architect delivered a 199-line review (`docs/review/REVIEW-2026-08-08-swebench-solver.md`) with 12 findings ranked by material impact. Every finding verified against the code by grep before touching. The Architect chose Path A on F1: rewire the matrix through `swebench_solver_topology`. This close records the seven fixes landed; F2 (recall@k) + F3 (tied to F1) + F4 (K=1 repro) + F5 (Django) + F11 (model names) + F12 (SoTA anchor) are noted below as follow-up.
- **F1 (load-bearing, load-bearing) — matrix wired through the wrong topology:** `swebench_matrix._build_repair_arm_from_models` called `swebench_repair_topology` — no repro_gen, no repro_base_validate, no select_exec, no `select.select_patch` rerank. Sprints 155 and 158 would have been dead code in Pass 2. Fixed: renamed helper to `_build_solver_arm_from_payload`, reads the `PreparedPayload` off `case.payload`, calls `solver_topology_from_payload` (from `swebench_suite.py`, already used by `swebench_solver_arm`). All five matrix arms + the backward-compat `repair_arm` route through the shared helper. Requires Cases to have gone through `prepare_swebench_case` upstream — the confirmatory runner already does this at line 303-310, so the wire holds; a caller of `swebench_matrix_suite` with raw Cases will fail loud (KeyError) rather than silently degrade.
- **F9 — `SuspectElements` defined-and-dead:** the element localizer at `topologies/swebench_solver/localize_elements.py` emitted `SuspectElements` but no producer in `assemble.py` used it. Fixed: `swebench_solver_topology` now wires `element_localizer_factory` instead of `localizer_factory`. `SuspectElements` added to the producer's declared schemas. Non-Python + syntax-error targets still degrade to whole-file per the element localizer's docstring — no silent drops.
- **F6 — watchdog defaults swapped:** repair topology had `watchdog_seconds=600.0`, solver had `60.0`. A bare instantiation of the solver at defaults would guillotine before `select_exec` finished one candidate. Fixed: swapped.
- **F7 — firewall unittest parser too loose:** the pre-fix parser did `frag = "myapp"; any("myapp" in f for f in tp_files)` — substring match. A pre-existing test at `myapp/other/test_foo.py` passed the firewall whenever `test_patch` added ANY file under `myapp/`. The exact leak the firewall exists to catch. Fixed: parser now maps `module.sub.Class` → `module/sub.py` and checks FILE EQUALITY against `test_patch`'s added files. One-segment parenthesised groups fail closed. Regression pin at `test_swebench_firewall_parser.py::test_unittest_id_substring_leak_fails_closed_post_F7`.
- **F8 — `diff --git` header regex missed mixed-quote:** git emits one-side-quoted headers for renames where only one path contains special chars; the pre-fix regex matched bare-both and quoted-both only, so mixed-quote rename sections silently dropped from `filter_diff` and the model_patch graded not-resolved. Fixed: one regex covering all three shapes (bare, quoted, mixed-either-side). Regression pins at `test_assay_swebench_workspace.py::test_section_b_path_parses_mixed_quote_headers_post_F8`.
- **F10 — sixteen view-name string literals across `assemble.py`:** the exact contract-truth gap KIT_DIARY 33 named — a typo would only surface as a KeyError when the trigger's predicate fired. Fixed: six module-level `_VIEW_*` constants, sixteen call sites swapped by sed. A mis-typed constant is now a NameError at import.
- **Tests updated:** two pre-fix tests baked in the substring behavior (`test_assay_swebench.py::test_firewall_check_both_test_id_formats`, `test_swebench_firewall_parser.py::test_parseable_unittest_id_added_by_test_patch_passes`). Both now use file-equality fixtures + a second assertion pinning the leak-fails-closed shape.
- **Dual contract:** artifact — ruff + mypy clean on all touched files; 36 tests across the directly-affected suites (matrix + localize_elements + repro_base_validate + swebench_solver) + 19 firewall/workspace tests; full suite regression backgrounded (expect 715+ pass, 3 skip).

The over-thought design was a self-inflicted trap — the tests earned their keep before the code ever ran.

### close-the-loop Sprint 158 — repro 2×2 + Cohen's κ (2026-08-08)
- **Scope:** the number that decides whether the model-generated reproduction test is a trustworthy tiebreak (per docs/swebench/swebench-solver-design.md §5). Split into two sub-sprints to stay near hard rule 6: (158-schema) `CaseResult.reproduction: str = ""` + `project_reproduction_for_selected(record)` helper in `assay/run.py` — read once at `run_arm_on_case` from the winning slot's TestResults. (158a) `_repro_aggregate` + three new fields (`repro_2x2`, `repro_kappa`, `repro_agreement_rate`) on ArmReport, propagated through `_Mid`/`build_report`; `caseresult_from_row` reads the new column. (158b) confirmatory runner's `_row` accepts + persists `reproduction`; salvage path projects it from the cached record so a regrade doesn't drop the 2×2 signal.
- **Cohen's κ:** the honest number — κ = (p_o − p_e) / (1 − p_e) over the {resolved_and_passed, resolved_and_failed, reproduced_and_passed, reproduced_and_failed} 2×2, `other` verdicts excluded (no signal for the tiebreak question). Distinguishes two degenerate cases the tests pin: (a) trivially-passing repro (always says RESOLVED) — raw agreement inflated by base rate (e.g. 0.8) but κ = 0.0 (informative zero, no better than chance); (b) BOTH marginals collapsed (all rows in one cell) — κ genuinely undefined per Cohen 1960, returns None while agreement + 2×2 stay populated.
- **Dual contract:** artifact — ruff + mypy clean on all touched files (run.py + cells.py + report.py + confirmatory script + new test_assay_report.py); tests 12/12 (new test_assay_report.py) + 68/68 across every affected surface; full suite regression backgrounded.
- **Observation contract:** test coverage pins seven distinct 2×2 shapes (perfect agreement / trivially-passing / anti-agreement / other-excluded / all-other-none / empty-input / moderate) + a case-insensitive verdict tolerance + the end-to-end propagation through `build_report`. The trivially-passing-repro test is the specific failure mode KIT_DIARY 21 named on flask-4045 — now measurable as κ = 0 in the report line.
- **Rubber Duck Pass:** wrote a wrong test assertion (`kappa is None` on the trivially-passing case) that reflected the wrong mental model — turned out κ = 0.0 was the correct answer AND the more useful signal than "undefined" would have been. Test rewritten to assert κ = 0.0 with a rationale that names the raw-vs-corrected distinction as the metric's whole point. Same discipline as KIT_DIARY finding 6 (a plausible-and-cited claim can still be wrong; verify against the real behaviour).
- **Open:** Sprint 159 (arm matrix + matched compute) next — including the `baseline_matched_compute` fifth arm + `ArmReport.resolve_per_call` efficiency-frontier field. Multi-round barrier deferred until 159 adds a `round` field to TestResults.

### close-the-loop Sprint 155 + review-fold pass 2 (2026-08-08) — base-fails-first repro validator + 2 CONFIRMED findings folded
- **Scope (155):** new producer `repro_base_validate` (`src/substrate/topologies/swebench_solver/repro_base_validate.py`) + `assemble.py` wiring + 6-test observation contract. Runs the model-generated repro ONCE on unmodified base_checkout; overwrites `ReproductionTest.code = ""` when the base run says RESOLVED (trivially-passing — the KIT_DIARY 21 flask-4045 false-positive shape) or OTHER (broken repro — the widening beyond the roadmap card's wording, intentional and documented). Passes through when the base says REPRODUCED (repro discriminates) or on any runner exception (death-resilience per KIT_DIARY 16). Empty-code passthrough emits a placeholder — LOAD-BEARING for the select_exec barrier (see fold pass 2).
- **Fold pass 2 (reviewer subagent):** two CONFIRMED findings + four nits landed on top of the same-turn 155 close. The two loads:
  - **B1 (concurrency race, load-bearing):** `select_exec` fired on every `Solved` with `predicate=True` and read the then-current reproduction view. A fast REPAIR round could beat the slow base-validation Docker call → SELECT reads the un-validated repro. The exact KIT_DIARY 21 shape the sprint was opened to defuse. Fixed: subscribe to `{Solved, ReproductionTest}`, predicate barriers on `_solved_round is not None AND len(reproduction) >= 2 AND len(test_results) == 0`. Whichever event lands last flips the gate true. Empty-code passthrough emit at repro_base_validate.py:71-77 becomes load-bearing (removing it would deadlock the barrier on empty-repro instances); documented in the docstring. Multi-round (`max_rounds=2`) degrades to single-round only under this gate — TestResults doesn't carry a `round` field (records.py:99-107 pre-existing); deferred.
  - **A1 (silent orphan `params_by_arm` keys, fold-pass audit):** `arms_fingerprint` silently ignored `params_by_arm` keys with no matching arm. A caller who renames a runner-side arm without rekeying the dict would drop both sides to `{name, role}` and pass the arms_hash check under UNPINNED params. Fixed: `arms_fingerprint` raises `ValueError` on any stale key with the specific stale-key names in the message. Two new tests pin the raise both directly and through `guard`.
- **Nits landed:** A3 (`mkdir` moved AFTER pre-reg gate → PREG failure genuinely leaves zero disk artifacts); B3 (adversarial "both markers" test — `reproduction_status` tests "Issue resolved" FIRST, so a repro with both markers is classified RESOLVED and demoted — deterministic tiebreak, now pinned); B7 (docstring line-ref drift 551 → assemble.py select_exec input builder, corrected); B8 (`_RunnerLike` protocol kwarg rename `files` → `extra_files` to match the real `DockerTestRunner.run` signature at select_exec.py:37-39 — harmless today, a trap if anyone calls by keyword).
- **Deferred (open):** B4 (empty-code emit "noise") — kept because it's load-bearing for the B1 barrier; documented rather than removed. Multi-round barrier gap — needs a `round` field on TestResults; deferred until Sprint 159's arm matrix work.
- **Dual contract:** artifact — ruff + mypy clean on all touched files (4 source + tests); Sprint 155 tests 7/7 (6 original + 1 adversarial-markers); preregistration tests 22/22 (20 pre-fold + 2 A1 pins); swebench_solver test suite 32/32 (barrier didn't break existing solver tests); full suite regression backgrounded.
- **Rubber Duck Pass:** the fold-pass reviewer earned its keep on B1 exactly the way KIT_DIARY finding 5 predicts — the concurrency race was live in the code with no test forcing it, my own confidence-in-race-being-benign was wrong, and the reviewer caught the mechanism from a code read. Same posture as Wave-5's double-finalise blocker.

### close-the-loop Phase B review-fold pass (2026-08-08) — adversarial subagent, 3 CONFIRMED sprint-151 bugs + 1 sprint-152 coverage gap + 2 nits on 146a
- **Scope:** reviewer spun up per the Architect's every-4-5-sprints cadence caught three load-bearing sprint-151 defects and one sprint-152 coverage gap. All CONFIRMED findings folded before the next sprint touches; nits folded alongside.
- **Folds:**
  - **151-#1 (arms_fingerprint scope drift, load-bearing):** original hashed only `{name, role}` — a same-name reroll (N=3 → N=5, or a model swap) would pass `check_arms_match` silently, the exact guarantee the docstring advertised. Fixed: `arms_fingerprint(arms, *, params_by_arm=)` accepts per-arm `{models, n, max_rounds}`; the runner threads them in; new regression test `test_arms_fingerprint_changes_when_arm_params_change` pins all three axes + `test_guard_rejects_reroll_of_arm_params_via_params_by_arm` closes the guard entrypoint.
  - **151-#3 (gate ordering bug):** `preregistration_guard` ran AFTER `meta.json` + `.cases.json` writes, so a gate failure leaked partial state to disk AND a PREG-on/off resume would splice mismatched-fingerprint cells past Gap 4's mixed-config guard. Fixed: gate runs FIRST (before fingerprint, meta, sidecar); on success the comparator merges into cfg and the fingerprint is computed once; meta.json is written once.
  - **151-#2 (third parallel `_fingerprint` with divergent canonical bytes):** `preregistration._canonical_bytes` used `ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":")`; `bench_coding.py` + `assay_swebench_confirmatory.py` used bare `json.dumps(cfg, sort_keys=True)` (defaults). Different bytes → different [:12] hashes on the same input, contradicting the docstring's consolidation claim. Fixed: `_canonical_bytes` aligned to bare defaults; `fingerprint()` promoted to a public helper; both bench scripts now import it; new test `test_fingerprint_matches_bare_json_dumps_bytes` pins byte-identity against the pre-existing scripts.
  - **152-#1 (delta/verdict/CI path never exercised on SWE-bench dispatch):** `test_report_from_cells_dispatches_swebench_end_to_end` used one arm that was its own control, so `delta_vs_control`/`ci_low`/`equivalence` were all None. Added `test_swebench_two_arm_report_exercises_delta_verdict_and_ci` — two arms, control never resolves, candidate always resolves; asserts `delta_vs_control == 1.0`, `discordant_arm_only == 6`, `ci_low > 0`, `equivalence == "superior"` through the full SWE-bench dispatch path.
  - **146a nit 1 (over-gating of constants check):** `test_constants_verify_against_installed_swebench` was behind the Docker + opt-in gate, so a swebench-package rename would live silently in every ordinary CI run. Split it out — now skips only when `swebench` is not importable; the two Docker-run tests keep the `@pytest.mark.swebench_harness` gate.
  - **146a nit 2 (docstring line reference confusion):** `test_empty_patch_does_not_grade_resolved` cited "swebench.py:295" ambiguously (upstream vs this repo). Rewritten to name the file unambiguously.
- **Dual contract:** artifact — ruff + mypy clean on every touched file. Preregistration tests 20/20 (5 new); cells tests 12/12 (1 new end-to-end delta test); stats + harness-binding + coding unchanged (17 + 3 skipped + 8). Full suite regression backgrounded.
- **Rubber Duck Pass:** the reviewer earned its keep on 151 exactly the way finding 5 of the KIT_DIARY predicted (adversarial reader grounded in the roadmap catches what my own tests structurally can't — none of the 15 preregistration tests varied N or models, so 151-#1 was invisible to the green suite). Sprint 150 came back clean under all six review headings including the Tango/Nam derivation cross-check (variance formula, bisection interval, sign convention, zero-discordant boundary all confirmed) — the score-TOST is defensible.
- **Deferred (open):** finding 152-#2 (sidecar loader reads 2 of 6 fields, docstring oversells) — the extra fields are for substrate-ui U-1 to render; deferring the fold until U-1 lands so the fields aren't dead cargo. Finding 151-#4 (hard-rule-12 "why (revised)" note) — this Sprint tail entry IS the note.

### game_of_life — tick-based simulation (2026-06-22, closed) — Conway's Life
- **Scope:** the "simulation" What-you-can-build sketch as Conway's Game of Life — many cell Producers acting each generation against a shared world-state Producer. `game_of_life/__init__.py` + test + committed record + bundled registration. Auto-band.

- **Rubber Duck Pass:** narrated the record in its own terms — `world` emits Generation(0) then 25 CellTick(0); 25 `cell` Producers compute concurrently; the `step` predicate (count CellNext at this tick == 25) fired exactly once per generation (no premature step, no double-step — order held); termination on the (generations+1)th Generation. Six categories: no missing pair / order violation / vocabulary gap (app kinds Producer-declared) / payload anomaly / timing surprise; tone n/a. Determinism: `first_divergence(a,b)` = None. Disposition: clean.
- **Closed:** clean (working-tree; uncommitted at this writing).

Added a `_terminated` guard at the top of `_cycle` + `_control.clear()` in `_view_failure`; test proves exactly one RunFinalised, last frame, dense seq, incl. a view raising on a control event (TriggerFired).
  - *BLOCKER "$blob nested inside resolved_input"* → **CONFIRMED, resolved-here**: oversized TriggerFired input was placed as the value of `resolved_input` instead of the top-level `$blob` field (locked schema requires exactly-one-of). Refactored to `_resolved_input_fields` returning the discriminated fragment; test asserts the mutual-exclusivity.
  - *MAJOR "cooldown counts raw seq not subscription-matched cycles"* (kernel §6) → resolved-here: now counts subscription-matching appends per trigger (`_trigger_match_count`).
  - *MAJOR "double cooldown for WhileTrue+Logical"* → resolved-here: `WhileTrue` no longer self-throttles; the builder lifts its cooldown to the trigger level; single enforcement point.
  - *MAJOR "hash from unsealed object"* → **investigated, original behavior is correct**: `msgspec.to_builtins` cannot encode the sealed `MappingProxyType` (verified); hashing the pre-seal value yields identical canonical bytes (JCS folds dict/MappingProxyType identically), so `input_sha256` is honest. Comment added.
  - *MINOR* (lifecycle payloads bypass the whitelist; PredicateQuarantined emits union fields) → deferred to Drift watchlist.
  - Second pass verified all five fixes HOLD, no new issues.

## Single-writer-per-section discipline

| Section | Who writes | What they write |
|---|---|---|
| `## Surfaced for review` | Agent + Architect | Halts (Agent), comprehension affirmations (Agent), partial verdicts (Agent), proposed decisions (Agent), specific feedback (Architect) |
| `## Decisions` | Architect ONLY (append-only) | Project scope; binding decisions; resolutions of halts |
| `## Built` | Agent (append-only) | One paragraph per sprint close |
| `## Deferred` | Anyone | Items deferred with re-visit condition |
| `## Open questions` | Anyone | Questions not yet answered |
| `## Drift watchlist` | Agent maintains | Observations to track across sprints |
| `## Sprint tail` | Agent maintains | Last 10 sprint closes |
